  <entry>
    <id>tag:github.com,2008:Repository/2312977/v5.0.1</id>
    <updated>2026-06-18T07:54:34Z</updated>
    <link rel="alternate" type="text/html" href="https://github.com/oldj/SwitchHosts/releases/tag/v5.0.1"/>
    <title>SwitchHosts v5.0.1</title>
    <content type="html">&lt;h3&gt;New Features&lt;/h3&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Added â€œLightweight Modeâ€: closing the main window can now release WebView memory, useful for users who want lower background resource usage.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Restored custom data storage location: choose a data directory, migrate existing data, and reset back to the default location.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Added a privileged background Helper on macOS to reduce repeated admin password prompts when writing to &lt;code&gt;/etc/hosts&lt;/code&gt;. (If the Helper has issues, you can open macOS â€œLogin Itemsâ€ settings directly to approve or re-enable it.)&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Added a â€œQuit app when closing the main windowâ€ option, better matching Windows usersâ€™ expectations.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;
&lt;h3&gt;Experience Improvements&lt;/h3&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Optimized tray window creation and destruction to reduce resource usage while running in the background.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Long update changelogs are now scrollable instead of being truncated.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Added a title to the Import URL dialog; modal titles are now bold globally for better readability.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Added a default path tooltip to the data directory recovery dialog to help avoid accidental actions.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Reordered items in General settings and improved multilingual copy.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;
&lt;h3&gt;Bug Fixes&lt;/h3&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Fixed an issue where the Windows/Linux title-bar close button did not correctly trigger Lightweight Mode memory release.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Fixed an issue where the tray mini window could close immediately after opening on Windows/Linux.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Fixed an issue where the main window could still appear on macOS login even when â€œHide at launchâ€ was enabled.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Strengthened the recovery flow for custom data directories when they are damaged, missing, or referenced by an invalid pointer.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Fixed a brief flash of default data that could appear during data directory recovery.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Fixed a release workflow race condition where multiple build jobs could create the draft Release concurrently.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;
&lt;h3&gt;Dependencies and Internal Improvements&lt;/h3&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Updated multiple frontend, backend, and Rust dependencies, including Electron, Vite, Hono, Axios, Lodash, React, Mantine, Vitest, Sass, and more.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Updated security-related indirect dependencies, including &lt;code&gt;picomatch&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code&gt;@xmldom/xmldom&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code&gt;follow-redirects&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code&gt;rustls-webpki&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code&gt;tar&lt;/code&gt;, and more.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Added test coverage for window lifecycle, Lightweight Mode, tray behavior, data directory recovery, and configuration update failure paths.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Improved macOS Helper diagnostic logging, making background component issues easier to inspect through system logs.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;
&lt;hr&gt;

    <id>tag:github.com,2008:Repository/2312977/v5.0.1-beta.8</id>
    <updated>2026-06-10T02:54:36Z</updated>
    <link rel="alternate" type="text/html" href="https://github.com/oldj/SwitchHosts/releases/tag/v5.0.1-beta.8"/>
    <title>SwitchHosts v5.0.1-beta.8</title>
    <content type="html">&lt;h2&gt;v5.0.1-beta.8&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;h3&gt;Added&lt;/h3&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Restored custom data storage location support, with options to change or reset the data directory in Advanced settings.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Added an option to copy existing data when changing the data directory, with a warning if the target already contains data.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Added a startup recovery dialog when the custom data directory is missing, unavailable, or has a damaged location record.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;
&lt;h3&gt;Improved&lt;/h3&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Update dialog changelogs now scroll instead of being truncated.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Added a title to the import-from-URL dialog and improved modal title styling.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;
&lt;h3&gt;Fixed&lt;/h3&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Fixed cases where SwitchHosts could accidentally fall back to the default data directory when the custom location was unavailable.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Blocked data writes, remote refresh, HTTP API, tray mini window, and related actions from bypassing the recovery flow.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Added stronger validation before switching data directories to avoid restarting into an unusable location.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;
&lt;h3&gt;Dependencies&lt;/h3&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Updated Rust dependency &lt;code&gt;tar&lt;/code&gt; from &lt;code&gt;0.4.45&lt;/code&gt; to &lt;code&gt;0.4.46&lt;/code&gt;.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;
&lt;hr&gt;
&lt;h2&gt;v5.0.1-beta.8&lt;/h2&gt;

    <id>tag:github.com,2008:Repository/2312977/v5.0.1-beta.7</id>
    <updated>2026-06-05T00:38:30Z</updated>
    <link rel="alternate" type="text/html" href="https://github.com/oldj/SwitchHosts/releases/tag/v5.0.1-beta.7"/>
    <title>SwitchHosts v5.0.1-beta.7</title>
    <content type="html">&lt;h1&gt;Changelog&lt;/h1&gt;
&lt;h2&gt;Added&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Added a macOS background helper that can write to &lt;code&gt;/etc/hosts&lt;/code&gt; silently after one-time authorization, reducing repeated password prompts during automatic remote hosts updates.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Added background helper management in General settings, including status, install, reinstall, and remove actions.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Added an â€œOpen System Settingsâ€ button to help users approve the helper in macOS Login Items.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;
&lt;h2&gt;Improved&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;macOS hosts applying now tries the background helper first, then automatically falls back to the existing system authorization flow if the helper is unsupported, unavailable, or fails.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Added safety checks to the helper write flow and restricted privileged writes to &lt;code&gt;/etc/hosts&lt;/code&gt; only.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Improved macOS login startup behavior to avoid unexpectedly showing a previously hidden main window.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;
&lt;h2&gt;Fixed&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Fixed background helper XPC signature validation failures on macOS 26.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Fixed possible concurrent helper unregister/register operations when repeatedly triggering helper repair.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;Fixed macOS universal builds failing to package the background helper binary correctly.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;
&lt;hr&gt;

    <id>tag:github.com,2008:Repository/2312977/v5.0.1-beta.4</id>
    <updated>2026-05-28T13:16:58Z</updated>
    <link rel="alternate" type="text/html" href="https://github.com/oldj/SwitchHosts/releases/tag/v5.0.1-beta.4"/>
    <title>SwitchHosts v5.0.1-beta.4</title>
    <content type="html">&lt;h2&gt;What&#39;s Changed&lt;/h2&gt;
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Full Changelog&lt;/strong&gt;: &lt;a class=&quot;commit-link&quot; href=&quot;https://github.com/oldj/SwitchHosts/compare/v5.0.0...v5.0.1-beta.4&quot;&gt;&lt;tt&gt;v5.0.0...v5.0.1-beta.4&lt;/tt&gt;&lt;/a&gt;&lt;/p&gt;</content>
    <author>
      <name>github-actions[bot]</name>
    </author>
    <media:thumbnail height="30" width="30" url="https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/in/15368?s=60&amp;v=4"/>
  </entry>
  <entry>
    <id>tag:github.com,2008:Repository/2312977/v5.0.0</id>
    <updated>2026-05-20T00:11:33Z</updated>
    <link rel="alternate" type="text/html" href="https://github.com/oldj/SwitchHosts/releases/tag/v5.0.0"/>
    <title>SwitchHosts v5.0.0</title>
    <content type="html">&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Update List&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt;
&lt;ul&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Core Architecture Migrated to Tauri 2&lt;/strong&gt;: Migrated from an Electron main process to a Rust/Tauri backend, added the complete &lt;code&gt;src-tauri&lt;/code&gt; implementation, and removed the old Electron main process code, packaging scripts, and electron-builder workflow.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;New v5 Data Structure&lt;/strong&gt;: Data is now stored in separate layers: &lt;code&gt;manifest.json&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code&gt;entries/&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code&gt;trashcan.json&lt;/code&gt;, &lt;code&gt;internal/config.json&lt;/code&gt;, and &lt;code&gt;internal/histories/&lt;/code&gt;. README backup instructions were updated accordingly.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Compatible Migration, Import, and Export&lt;/strong&gt;: Supports first-run migration from legacy PotDb data; manual import now supports v3, v4, and v5 backups; exports now use the v5 backup JSON format with timestamped filenames.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;System Hosts Writing Reworked&lt;/strong&gt;: Switched to native system privilege flows: Security.framework on macOS, &lt;code&gt;pkexec&lt;/code&gt; on Linux, and UAC self-elevation on Windows. The in-app sudo password input was removed.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;New/Reworked Auto Update System&lt;/strong&gt;: Integrated Tauri updater, with background update checks, manual download/install, proxy support for update requests, and generated/published &lt;code&gt;latest.json&lt;/code&gt;.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Rebuilt CI and Release Pipeline&lt;/strong&gt;: Added GitHub Actions CI and Release workflows. Releases now run the full test suite before publishing and support signed/validated builds for macOS universal/arm64/x64, Windows x64/x86/arm64, and Linux x64/arm64.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Editor Upgraded to CodeMirror 6&lt;/strong&gt;: Improved large-file performance, syntax highlighting, line numbers, read-only mode, history snapshot rendering, and cursor/display stability.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Enhanced Remote Hosts Refresh&lt;/strong&gt;: Added startup refresh support, response size limits, and CRLF/LF normalization.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Improved System Tray Behavior&lt;/strong&gt;: Fixed macOS focus handling, multi-monitor positioning, rounded corners, and interaction issues.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Major Main UI Redesign&lt;/strong&gt;: Added a left activity bar, Hosts/Trashcan view switching, resizable left and right panels, a right-side details panel, centered title bar, frameless window, and cross-platform window controls.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Right Details Panel&lt;/strong&gt;: Shows the current hosts type, rule count, remote URL, refresh interval, last refresh time, group contents, and folder selection mode; trashcan items can be restored or permanently deleted from the panel.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Unified Trashcan Experience&lt;/strong&gt;: Moved the trashcan entry to the left sidebar, with item count display, restore, permanent delete, clear, and details panel operations.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Preferences Reworked&lt;/strong&gt;: Split preferences into General, Commands, Proxy, and Advanced tabs. General and Advanced settings auto-save, while Commands and Proxy keep manual save status feedback.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;New Preference Options&lt;/strong&gt;: Added system theme by default, launch at login, and refresh remote hosts on startup.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Enhanced Proxy Support&lt;/strong&gt;: Added &lt;code&gt;socks5&lt;/code&gt; proxy protocol support, and both remote hosts refresh and app updates can now use proxy settings.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Local HTTP API Preserved and Migrated&lt;/strong&gt;: Reimplemented the &lt;code&gt;50761&lt;/code&gt; API with Rust/Axum, supporting &lt;code&gt;/api/list&lt;/code&gt; and &lt;code&gt;/api/toggle&lt;/code&gt;; when port binding fails, the setting is rolled back and the user is notified.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Localization and Copy Updates&lt;/strong&gt;: Organized and sorted locale keys, fixed language preference fallback display, localized menus and find window text, and updated multilingual README screenshots and v5 documentation.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Stability Fixes&lt;/strong&gt;: Fixed concurrent config writes, HTTP API startup failure state, invisible window after hiding the Dock icon, stuck hover state after drag, titlebar/tray switch synchronization, long title truncation, loading screen lockups, and more.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Expanded Test Coverage&lt;/strong&gt;: Added Playwright e2e tests, Rust tests, and frontend component/model unit tests covering preferences, import/export, remote hosts, local hosts, trashcan, title bar, tree operations, find/replace, and more.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Toolchain Upgrades&lt;/strong&gt;: Upgraded to Vite 8, TypeScript 6, Mantine 9, Vitest 4, and Tauri 2.11, and introduced ESLint 10 with naming convention rules.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Development and Build Command Changes&lt;/strong&gt;: Development now uses &lt;code&gt;npm run tauri:dev&lt;/code&gt;; production builds use &lt;code&gt;npm run tauri:build&lt;/code&gt;; build artifacts are now located under &lt;code&gt;src-tauri/target/release/bundle/&lt;/code&gt;.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;li&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Removed Legacy Electron Content&lt;/strong&gt;: Removed &lt;code&gt;src/main/*&lt;/code&gt;, the Electron updater, old upload/packaging scripts, old main-process tests, and &lt;code&gt;vite.main.config.mts&lt;/code&gt;.&lt;/li&gt;
&lt;/ul&gt;
&lt;p&gt;&lt;strong&gt;æ›´æ–°åˆ—è¡¨&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/p&gt;
